> Note (15:04): the emulator was restarted with `-gpu host` (the same AVD, app data and pairing kept): with `swiftshader_indirect` under the host load (~90–110) the app's main thread waited in `ThreadedRenderer.syncAndDrawFrame` (render thread starved; `kill -3` trace) and Android showed "isn't responding". In this boot Wi-Fi has its routes (`table wlan0`: default via 10.0.2.2), so Wi-Fi stays on. The app reconnected by itself ("Connected to Test Node 458").
